# Mental-Health Clinician Tools — 1,000-Record Master Reference

Simple Markdown extraction of the workbook’s canonical `Master` sheet for engineering reference.

## Extraction notes

- Source workbook: `/Users/jack/Downloads/mental_health_clinician_tools_master_1000.xlsx`
- Workbook SHA-256: `50e56b724bfabc656dffb377563664dda1bc9b48ade1c29d972b23b1db35cd09`
- Extracted: `2026-08-22`
- Master records retained: **1,000**
- Tool/service/module/intervention records: **863**
- Vendor/company profile records: **137**
- No records were merged, removed, enriched, or rewritten.
- Spreadsheet row numbers, record IDs, canonical keys, statuses, evidence tiers, and source URLs are preserved.
- `Summary`, `YC & Emerging`, `Legacy & Acquired`, `Sources`, and `Methodology` are supplementary views or documentation and are not appended as additional tool rows.
- Duplicate sections below are mechanical candidates only; they are not merge decisions.
